true,\n\t\tServiceName:     \"muster\",\n\t\tServiceVersion:  \"1.0.0\",\n\t\tMetricsExporter: \"prometheus\",\n\t})\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create instrumentation: logger)),\n\t\toauth.WithSecurityEventRateLimiter(security.NewRateLimiter(DefaultSecurityEventRate, DefaultSecurityEventBurst, DefaultMetadataFetchBurst, logger)),\n\t}\n\n\tif len(cfg.KubernetesSATrusts) > 0 {\n\t\ttrusts := make([]oauthserver.KubernetesSATrust, len(cfg.KubernetesSATrusts))\n\t\tfor i, t := range t.AllowedServiceAccounts,\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\topts = append(opts, oauthserver.WithKubernetesSATrust(trusts))\n\t}\n\n\tif len(cfg.TrustedIssuers) > 0 {\n\t\tissuers :=
